Bash: Rework leg segments and rerun board
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/elements/deer-elk/src && python3 - <<'EOF'
s=open('rig.py').read()
old=s[s.index('    # upper segment: forearm / gaskin'):s.index('    for t in out:\n        t.leg = name')]
new='''    # upper segment: forearm / gaskin, deep fore-and-aft at the top (muscle), running up into
    # the body; lateral radius smaller, so the leg is a blade from the side and slim from the front
    top = P[0] + np.array([0, 0, 0.08 if fore else 0.12])
    seg1 = np.array([top, P[0], P[0] * 0.6 + P[1] * 0.4, P[0] * 0.25 + P[1] * 0.75, P[1]])
    if fore:
        ra1 = np.array([r[0] * 1.25, r[0] * 1.1, r[0] * 0.9, r[1] * 1.05, r[1]])
    else:
        ra1 = np.array([r[0] * 1.6, r[0] * 1.35, r[0] * 0.95, r[1] * 1.1, r[1]])
    out.append(tube_from_points(name + "1", seg1, ra1, ra1 * 0.62, [1, 0, 0], "leg1", n=12))
    # hock point: the calcaneus sticks out behind the hock
    if not fore:
        d = P[1] - P[0]
        d /= np.linalg.norm(d) + 1e-9
        back = np.array([-1.0, 0, 0])
        hp = P[1] + back * r[1] * 1.0 + np.array([0, 0, r[1] * 0.4])
        out.append(tube_from_points(name + "hk", np.array([P[1] - d * r[1] * 2.2, hp]), [r[1] * 0.8, r[1] * 0.45], [r[1] * 0.5, r[1] * 0.35], [0, 0, 1], "leg", thin=True, n=4))
    # knee (carpus) knob on the foreleg
    if fore:
        out.append(tube_from_points(name + "kn", np.array([P[1] + np.array([0, 0, 0.02]), P[1] - np.array([0, 0, 0.03])]),
                                    [r[1] * 1.15, r[1] * 1.0], [r[1] * 0.75, r[1] * 0.7], [1, 0, 0], "leg", thin=True, n=4))
    # cannon: slim, flat-sided
    seg2 = np.array([P[1], (P[1] + P[2]) / 2, P[2]])
    out.append(tube_from_points(name + "2", seg2, [r[1] * 0.85, r[2], r[2] * 1.05], [r[1] * 0.6, r[2] * 0.72, r[2] * 0.75], [1, 0, 0], "leg", thin=True, n=8))
    # fetlock knob + pastern
    d3 = P[3] - P[2]
    L3n = np.linalg.norm(d3) + 1e-9
    seg3 = np.array([P[2], P[2] + d3 * 0.5, P[3] - d3 / L3n * r[4] * 0.8])
    out.append(tube_from_points(name + "3", seg3, [r[3], r[2] * 0.85, r[2] * 0.95], [r[3] * 0.75, r[2] * 0.65, r[2] * 0.75], [1, 0, 0], "leg", thin=True, n=6))
    # hoof: a small dark wedge, toe forward, flat sole
    hb = P[3] - d3 / L3n * r[4] * 1.2
    toe = P[3] + np.array([r[4] * 1.0, 0, -r[4] * 0.35])
    out.append(tube_from_points(name + "hf", np.array([hb, P[3], toe]), [r[4] * 0.9, r[4] * 0.8, r[4] * 0.35], [r[4] * 0.7, r[4] * 0.7, r[4] * 0.4],
                                [0, 0, 1], "hoof", thin=True, n=5))
'''
s=s.replace(old,new)
open('rig.py','w').write(s)

            out[u > 0.72] = HOOF''','''        if seg == "hf":
            out[:] = HOOF''')

cat > sizes.py <<'EOF'
"""The real test: photo reduced by area-averaging | mine, at 60, 30, 16, 8 px body length (4x zoom)."""
import numpy as np, sys
from PIL import Image
import rig, paint, board
from render import View
from board import BOARD
tag = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 'x'
rows = []
for i, (f, crop, gr, wr, (spn, sex), g, ph, yaw) in enumerate(BOARD):
    im = Image.open(f).convert('L').crop(crop)
    Hphoto = gr - wr
    cells = []
    for L in (60, 30, 16, 8):
        # photo: scale so that shoulder height = L/1.62 px (the same convention as mine)
        s = (L / 1.62) / Hphoto
        p = im.resize((max(1, int(im.width * s)), max(1, int(im.height * s))), Image.BOX)
        ind = rig.Individual(spn, sex, 'adult', 'autumn', 1)
        R, _, k = paint.paint_index(ind, g, ph, View(yaw=yaw, elev=0, sun_el=50, sun_az=-140, ss=8), size_px=L)
        gimg = paint.grey(R, bg=0.92)
        cells.append((np.array(p), gimg))
    rows.append(cells)
Z = 4
tiles = []
for cells in rows:
    hh = max(max(a.shape[0], b.shape[0]) for a, b in cells)
    ww = sum(a.shape[1] + b.shape[1] + 6 for a, b in cells)
    t = np.full((hh, ww), 255, np.uint8)
    x = 0
    for a, b in cells:
        t[hh - a.shape[0]:, x:x + a.shape[1]] = a; x += a.shape[1] + 2
        t[hh - b.shape[0]:, x:x + b.shape[1]] = b; x += b.shape[1] + 4
    tiles.append(t)
W = max(t.shape[1] for t in tiles)
out = np.full((sum(t.shape[0] + 3 for t in tiles), W), 255, np.uint8)
y = 0
for t in tiles:
    out[y:y + t.shape[0], :t.shape[1]] = t; y += t.shape[0] + 3
Image.fromarray(np.kron(out, np.ones((Z, Z), np.uint8))).save(f'../stages/s1/sizes_{tag}.png')
EOF

"""Palettes: (material, step) -> RGB for a coat, an hour and a depth.

The palette is the light. Every ramp walks in OKLab from a cool dark (pulled toward the sky's
ambient colour) to a warm light (pulled toward the sun's colour); golden hour and dusk pull harder
and lower the key. Hide colours were read off the reference photos (the Roosevelt cow and bull in
refs/roosevelt_elk_cow, the blacktails in the naturalist's photos).
"""
import numpy as np
import rig

STEPS = 6


def _lin(c):
    c = np.asarray(c, float) / 255
    return np.where(c > 0.04045, ((c + 0.055) / 1.055) ** 2.4, c / 12.92)


def _srgb(l):
    l = np.clip(l, 0, 1)
    return np.where(l > 0.0031308, 1.055 * l ** (1 / 2.4) - 0.055, 12.92 * l) * 255


M1 = np.array([[0.4122214708, 0.5363325363, 0.0514459929], [0.2119034982, 0.6806995451, 0.1073969566],
               [0.0883024619, 0.2817188376, 0.6299787005]])
M2 = np.array([[0.2104542553, 0.7936177850, -0.0040720468], [1.9779984951, -2.4285922050, 0.4505937099],
               [0.0259040371, 0.7827717662, -0.8086757660]])
M2i = np.linalg.inv(M2)
M1i = np.linalg.inv(M1)


def to_lab(c):
    return M2 @ np.cbrt(M1 @ _lin(c))


def to_rgb(lab):
    return tuple(int(round(v)) for v in _srgb(M1i @ ((M2i @ lab) ** 3)))


def mix(a, b, t):
    return to_rgb(to_lab(a) * (1 - t) + to_lab(b) * t)


def ramp(dark, light, n, sun, amb, warm=0.22, cool=0.3, key=1.0):
    out = []
    Ls, La = to_lab(sun), to_lab(amb)
    for i in range(n):
        t = i / max(n - 1, 1)
        c = to_lab(dark) * (1 - t) + to_lab(light) * t
        c = c * (1 - warm * t ** 1.5) + Ls * warm * t ** 1.5
        c = c * (1 - cool * (1 - t) ** 2) + La * cool * (1 - t) ** 2
        c = c.copy()
        c[0] *= key
        out.append(to_rgb(c))
    return out


# hours: sun colour, ambient (sky) colour, key, sun elevation, a sky colour and a ground ramp
HOURS = {
    "dawn":     dict(sun=(255, 196, 150), amb=(96, 104, 150), key=0.80, el=10, warm=0.30, cool=0.40, sky=(170, 170, 196)),
    "morning":  dict(sun=(255, 236, 200), amb=(120, 150, 190), key=0.96, el=30, warm=0.2, cool=0.30, sky=(170, 200, 226)),
    "noon":     dict(sun=(255, 250, 236), amb=(130, 160, 196), key=1.0, el=58, warm=0.14, cool=0.26, sky=(160, 196, 232)),
    "golden":   dict(sun=(255, 196, 120), amb=(110, 110, 160), key=0.92, el=14, warm=0.36, cool=0.34, sky=(236, 196, 150)),
    "dusk":     dict(sun=(250, 150, 120), amb=(70, 70, 130), key=0.66, el=4, warm=0.40, cool=0.48, sky=(150, 120, 160)),
    "overcast": dict(sun=(214, 216, 214), amb=(150, 156, 160), key=0.86, el=45, warm=0.06, cool=0.22, sky=(196, 200, 204)),
}

# coats: material -> (shadow dark, lit light). Read off the photos.

    ("elk", "f"): {
        rig.BODY: ((96, 70, 48), (212, 186, 146)),      # cow: pale greyish tan
        rig.DARK: ((40, 28, 22), (126, 94, 66)),        # cow's neck: mid chocolate

SUMMER = {
    ("deer", "f"): {rig.BODY: ((98, 50, 26), (214, 132, 72)), rig.LEG: ((92, 52, 30), (206, 134, 80)),
                    rig.DARK: ((50, 30, 20), (130, 80, 50)), rig.BELLY: ((120, 90, 64), (222, 190, 150))},
    ("elk", "f"): {rig.BODY: ((104, 60, 34), (214, 150, 94)), rig.DARK: ((40, 26, 18), (120, 80, 52))},
    ("elk", "m"): {rig.BODY: ((104, 56, 30), (216, 140, 84))},
}
YOUNG = {  # calves and fawns: red-brown with cream spots
    "elk": {rig.BODY: ((94, 50, 28), (204, 132, 80)), rig.DARK: ((50, 30, 20), (140, 88, 56)), rig.LEG: ((70, 40, 24), (170, 110, 70))},
    "deer": {rig.BODY: ((96, 50, 26), (210, 128, 70)), rig.LEG: ((90, 50, 28), (200, 130, 78))},
}

GROUND, GSHADOW, CONTACT, SKY, GRASSDARK = 20, 21, 22, 23, 24
NROW = 25


def coat_table(ind):
    key = (ind.species, ind.sex if ind.age == "adult" else "f")
    t = dict(COATS[key])
    if ind.season == "summer":
        t.update(SUMMER.get((ind.species, ind.sex if ind.age == "adult" else "f"), SUMMER.get((ind.species, "f"), {})))
    if ind.age == "young":
        t.update(YOUNG[ind.species])
    if ind.sex == "m" and ind.species == "elk" and ind.age != "adult":
        t = dict(COATS[("elk", "f")])
        t.update({rig.ANTLER: COATS[("elk", "m")][rig.ANTLER], rig.TIP: COATS[("elk", "m")][rig.TIP], rig.VELVET: COATS[("elk", "m")][rig.VELVET]})
    for m in range(15):
        if m not in t:
            t[m] = t[rig.BODY]
    return t


GRASS = {"summer": ((34, 58, 30), (150, 176, 84)), "autumn": ((58, 56, 34), (196, 176, 110)),
         "winter": ((48, 54, 40), (150, 146, 110)), "spring": ((30, 60, 30), (140, 190, 80))}


def palette(ind, hour="morning", depth=0.0, ground="summer", haze=None):
    """-> (NROW, STEPS, 3) uint8 table. depth in 0..1 lifts darks toward the haze (aerial perspective)."""
    h = HOURS[hour]
    tab = np.zeros((NROW, STEPS, 3), np.uint8)
    ct = coat_table(ind)
    haze = haze or h["sky"]
    for m, (d, l) in ct.items():
        r = ramp(d, l, STEPS, h["sun"], h["amb"], h["warm"], h["cool"], h["key"])
        tab[m] = [depth_mix(c, haze, depth, i / (STEPS - 1)) for i, c in enumerate(r)]
    gd, gl = GRASS[ground]
    g = ramp(gd, gl, STEPS, h["sun"], h["amb"], h["warm"], h["cool"], h["key"])
    tab[GROUND] = [depth_mix(c, haze, depth, i / (STEPS - 1)) for i, c in enumerate(g)]
    # cast shadow on the ground: the ground's own darks, cooler (ambient-lit)
    gs = ramp(gd, mix(gl, h["amb"], 0.45), STEPS, h["amb"], h["amb"], 0.0, 0.5, h["key"] * 0.82)
    tab[GSHADOW] = [depth_mix(c, haze, depth, i / (STEPS - 1)) for i, c in enumerate(gs)]
    tab[CONTACT] = [depth_mix(mix(gd, (10, 10, 14), 0.5), haze, depth, 0)] * STEPS
    tab[SKY] = [h["sky"]] * STEPS
    return tab


def depth_mix(c, haze, depth, t):
    if depth <= 0:
        return c
    # darks lift strongly toward the haze, lights barely (measured on Ferrari by the master-copy arm)
    w = depth * (0.85 - 0.55 * t)
    return mix(c, haze, min(w, 0.95))

def colour(R, ind, hour="morning", ground="summer", depth=0.0, bg=None, rng=None):
    """Composite the index canvas onto a plain meadow strip. Returns RGB uint8."""
    import palette as P
    tab = P.palette(ind, hour, depth, ground)
    H, W = R.mat.shape
    rng = rng or np.random.default_rng(0)
    if bg is None:
        # meadow: mid step with a sprinkle of lighter/darker ticks (not the subject of this study)
        g = np.full((H, W), 3, np.int32)
        n = rng.random((H, W))
        g[n < 0.18] = 2
        g[n > 0.85] = 4
        img = tab[P.GROUND][g]
        gs = R.ground_shadow > 0.45
        img[gs] = tab[P.GSHADOW][np.clip(g[gs] - 1, 0, 5)]
    else:
        img = bg.copy()
    on = R.mat >= 0
    img[on] = tab[R.mat[on], R.step[on]]
    return img
EOF

"""Colour: photo (area-reduced) | mine at 60, 30, 16, 8 px, 4x."""
import numpy as np, sys
from PIL import Image
import rig, paint
from render import View
from board import BOARD
tag = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 'x'
hour = sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 'morning'
rows = []
for i, (f, crop, gr, wr, (spn, sex), g, ph, yaw) in enumerate(BOARD):
    im = Image.open(f).convert('RGB').crop(crop)
    Hphoto = gr - wr
    cells = []
    for L in (60, 30, 16, 8):
        s = (L / 1.62) / Hphoto
        p = im.resize((max(1, int(im.width * s)), max(1, int(im.height * s))), Image.BOX)
        ind = rig.Individual(spn, sex, 'adult', 'autumn', 1)
        R, _, k = paint.paint_index(ind, g, ph, View(yaw=yaw, elev=4, sun_el=45, sun_az=-140, ss=8), size_px=L)
        cells.append((np.array(p), paint.colour(R, ind, hour)))
    rows.append(cells)
Z = 4
tiles = []
for cells in rows:
    hh = max(max(a.shape[0], b.shape[0]) for a, b in cells)
    ww = sum(a.shape[1] + b.shape[1] + 6 for a, b in cells)
    t = np.full((hh, ww, 3), 255, np.uint8)
    x = 0
    for a, b in cells:
        t[hh - a.shape[0]:, x:x + a.shape[1]] = a; x += a.shape[1] + 2
        t[hh - b.shape[0]:, x:x + b.shape[1]] = b; x += b.shape[1] + 4
    tiles.append(t)
W = max(t.shape[1] for t in tiles)
out = np.full((sum(t.shape[0] + 3 for t in tiles), W, 3), 255, np.uint8)
y = 0
for t in tiles:
    out[y:y + t.shape[0], :t.shape[1]] = t; y += t.shape[0] + 3
Image.fromarray(out.repeat(Z, 0).repeat(Z, 1)).save(f'../stages/s1/sizesc_{tag}.png')
EOF

"""The painter: an Individual + gait/pose + view + size -> index canvas -> RGB.

index canvas: mat (int, -1 = empty) and step (0..5). The palette turns (mat, step) into colour.
"""
import numpy as np
from dataclasses import dataclass, replace
import rig, render, coat
from render import View

GROUND = 20          # material ids beyond the hide: ground and its cast shadow
GSHADOW = 21
CONTACT = 22


def classes(R, view):
    """Form classes: light 5, 4 | halftone 3 || reflected 2 | shadow 1 | core 0."""
    v = R.lit
    n = R.nrm
    sh = R.shadow > 0.5
    step = np.zeros(v.shape, np.int32)
    litf = (v > 0.03) & ~sh
    step[litf & (v > 0.74)] = 5
    step[litf & (v <= 0.74) & (v > 0.36)] = 4
    step[litf & (v <= 0.36)] = 3
    shf = ~litf
    step[shf] = 1
    # core: just past the terminator on unshadowed form
    step[shf & ~sh & (v > -0.28)] = 0
    # reflected light: surfaces facing down take bounce from the lit ground; the far side takes sky
    bounce = n[..., 2] < -0.4
    step[shf & bounce] = 2
    return step


def paint_index(ind, gait="stand", phase=0.0, view=View(), size_px=60, pose=None, lines=True):
    sp = ind.spec()
    if pose is None:
        pose = rig.gait_pose(sp, gait, phase)
    tubes, sp = rig.build(ind, pose)
    # size_px: the adult elk-cow body length convention -> px per metre for THIS individual:
Bash: Continuous value field with seam dither
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/elements/deer-elk/src && python3 - <<'EOF'
s=open('paint.py').read()
i=s.index('def classes(R, view):'); j=s.index('def paint_index(')
new='''def value_field(R, view, tubes):
    """Continuous step coordinate x in [0, 5]: light 5, 4 | halftone 3 || reflected 2 | shadow 1 | core 0."""
    v = R.lit
    n = R.nrm
    sh = R.shadow > 0.5
    x = np.zeros(v.shape)
    litf = (v > 0.04) & ~sh
    x[litf] = 3.0 + 2.5 * np.clip((v[litf] - 0.04) / 0.8, 0, 1)
    shf = ~litf
    x[shf] = 1.1
    core = shf & ~sh & (v > -0.3)
    x[core] = 0.45
    # reflected light: downward-facing hide takes bounce from the lit ground
    bounce = np.clip(-n[..., 2] - 0.2, 0, 1)
    x[shf] += 1.0 * bounce[shf]
    # far-side limbs, ear and antler sit one step down: they are in the body's shade and behind it
    import render as RR
    f = RR.view_xf(view)
    tor = [t for t in tubes if t.name == "torso"][0]
    _, _, dmid = f(tor.c.mean(0))
    far_parts = set()
    for i, t in enumerate(tubes):
        if t.name[:2] in ("LF", "RF", "LH", "RH") or t.name.startswith(("ear", "beam", "tine", "shoulder", "ant", "fork", "brow")):
            _, _, d = f(t.c.mean(0))
            if d > dmid + 0.02:
                far_parts.add(i)
    far = np.isin(R.part, list(far_parts)) if far_parts else np.zeros(v.shape, bool)
    x[far] -= 1.1
    return np.clip(x, 0, 5), far


def quantize(x, dither=True):
    """Round the step coordinate; a narrow checker seam only where x sits near a half step
    (Ferrari mixes pure, 50%, pure, 1-2 px wide)."""
    fl = np.floor(x)
    fr = x - fl
    step = np.where(fr >= 0.5, fl + 1, fl)
    if dither:
        H, W = x.shape
        yy, xx = np.mgrid[0:H, 0:W]
        chk = ((yy + xx) & 1) == 1
        seam = (fr > 0.40) & (fr < 0.60)
        step = np.where(seam, np.where(chk, fl + 1, fl), step)
    return np.clip(step, 0, 5).astype(np.int32)

    return R, tubes, k''','''    x, far = value_field(R, view, tubes)
    R.x = x
    R.far = far
    R.step = quantize(x, dither=size_px >= 40)
    return R, tubes, k''')

Write: elements/deer-elk/src/s0_copy.py
"""Stage 0: copy Ferrari's sunlit warm boulder (Living Worlds V19, native (238,255)-(280,290)).

Ferrari never painted an elk. The closest passage in his work to a warm-coloured, rounded, sunlit
animal-sized mass is the orange boulder in front of the stone gate in V19: at 42x35 px it is the
size of a 60-px elk's body. What its indices show (printed by this script):

  lit face   c f checker over most of the face, pure f on the brightest crown, pure c where it turns
  turn       a 1-2 px band of 'a' (a dull rose, one step down), then '8', a saturated red-brown:
             the reflected/core colour is MORE saturated than the lit colours
  shadow     '7' as a 1-px line on the shadow-side silhouette; '2'/'0' for the cast shadow
  lit edge   against the lighter snow, the lit face meets the background directly (lost edge)

The copy paints the same silhouette from a dome built on the mask (distance transform ->
height -> normals), lights it, and quantizes the step coordinate with a wide checker band.
Only his silhouette and palette are borrowed; everything at pixel level is generated.
"""
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image
from scipy import ndimage as ndi
import lw

OUT = "../stages/s0/"


def main():
    import os
    os.makedirs(OUT, exist_ok=True)
    d = lw.load("V19")
    idx, pal = d["idx"], d["pal"]
    y0, y1, x0, x1 = 255, 290, 238, 280
    r = idx[y0:y1, x0:x1]
    # his classes for this rock, dark to light
    C = {"0": 89, "2": 94, "7": 93, "8": 100, "a": 85, "c": 99, "f": 98, "h": 74, "d": 75, "9": 76}
    body = np.isin(r, [C["c"], C["f"], C["a"], C["8"]])
    lab, n = ndi.label(body)
    sizes = ndi.sum(body, lab, range(1, n + 1))
    mask = lab == (1 + int(np.argmax(sizes)))
    mask = ndi.binary_fill_holes(mask)
    # dome from the mask
    dist = ndi.distance_transform_edt(mask)
    h = np.sqrt(np.clip(dist / dist.max(), 0, 1)) * dist.max() * 0.9
    h = ndi.gaussian_filter(h, 1.2)
    gy, gx = np.gradient(h)
    nrm = np.stack([-gx, gy, np.ones_like(h) * 1.4], -1)       # x right, y up, z toward viewer
    nrm /= np.linalg.norm(nrm, axis=-1, keepdims=True)
    L = np.array([0.55, 0.55, 0.62]); L /= np.linalg.norm(L)  # sun upper right, as in his scene
    v = (nrm * L).sum(-1)
    # step coordinate: 2 = '8' (core, saturated), 3 = 'a', 4 = 'c', 5 = 'f'
    x = np.where(v > 0.25, 4 + np.clip((v - 0.25) / 0.55, 0, 1.2), 2 + 2 * np.clip((v + 0.25) / 0.5, 0, 1))
    best = None
    for w in (0.2, 0.4, 0.6, 0.8):
        st = quant(x, w)
        cls = np.array([C["2"], C["7"], C["8"], C["a"], C["c"], C["f"]])
        mine = np.where(mask, cls[np.clip(st, 0, 5)], r)
        match = (mine[mask] == r[mask]).mean()
        print(f"checker band {w}: match {match:.2f}")
        if best is None or match > best[0]:
            best = (match, w, mine)
    match, w, mine = best
    # an edge rule found by reading: the shadow-side silhouette pixel is '8' then 'a' inside
    er = mask & ~ndi.binary_erosion(mask)
    shadow_side = er & (v < 0.35)
    mine2 = mine.copy()
    mine2[shadow_side] = C["8"]
    inner = mask & ~ndi.binary_erosion(mask, iterations=2) & ~er & (v < 0.45)
    mine2[inner] = C["a"]
    m2 = (mine2[mask] == r[mask]).mean()
    print(f"best band {w}: {match:.2f}; with the rim rule: {m2:.2f}")
    Z = 8
    a = pal[r]
    b = pal[mine2]
    gap = np.full((r.shape[0], 2, 3), 20, np.uint8)
    img = np.concatenate([a, gap, b], 1)
    Image.fromarray(img.repeat(Z, 0).repeat(Z, 1)).save(OUT + "s0_boulder_copy_8x.png")
    return m2


def quant(x, w):
    fl = np.floor(x)
    fr = x - fl
    H, W = x.shape
    yy, xx = np.mgrid[0:H, 0:W]
    chk = ((yy + xx) & 1) == 1
    lo, hi = 0.5 - w / 2, 0.5 + w / 2
    st = np.where(fr >= 0.5, fl + 1, fl)
    seam = (fr > lo) & (fr < hi)
    return np.where(seam, np.where(chk, fl + 1, fl), st).astype(int)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

    B = np.array([[0, 2], [3, 1]])

    face = mask & (dist > 2.5) & np.isin(r, [C["c"], C["f"]])
    yy, xx = np.nonzero(face)
    dens = ndi.uniform_filter((r == C["f"]).astype(float), 4)[face]
    A = np.stack([xx, yy, np.ones_like(xx)], 1).astype(float)
    coef, *_ = np.linalg.lstsq(A, dens, rcond=None)
    print("fitted f-density gradient per px (x, y, const):", np.round(coef, 3))
    Y, X = np.mgrid[0:r.shape[0], 0:r.shape[1]]
    xg = 4 + np.clip(coef[0] * X + coef[1] * Y + coef[2], 0, 1)
    xg = np.where(dist > 2.5, xg, x)
    for off in [(0, 0), (0, 1), (1, 0), (1, 1)]:
        st = bayer_quant(xg, off)
        cls = np.array([C["2"], C["7"], C["8"], C["a"], C["c"], C["f"]])
        mb = np.where(mask, cls[np.clip(st, 0, 5)], r)
        match = (mb[mask] == r[mask]).mean()

# output:
def paint_index(ind, gait="stand", phase=0.0, view=View(), size_px=60, pose=None, lines=True):
    sp = ind.spec()
    if pose is None:
        pose = rig.gait_pose(sp, gait, phase)
    tubes, sp = rig.build(ind, pose)
    # size_px: the adult elk-cow body length convention -> px per metre for THIS individual:
    # we size by the individual's own nose-to-rump length in a side view (so a 4-px deer is 4 px).
    k = size_px / (sp.H * 1.62)
    R = render.rasterize(tubes, ind, view, k)
    if lines:
        R = render.thin_lines(R, tubes, ind, view, k)
    on = (R.cov >= 0.45) | R.thin
    R.mat = np.where(on, R.mat, -1)
    x, far = value_field(R, view, tubes)
    R.x = x
    R.far = far
    R.step = quantize(x, dither=size_px >= 40)
    return R, tubes, k


GREY_BASE = {  # luminance of step 4 per material, 0..1 (values from the photos' hide, in grey)
Write: elements/deer-elk/src/hide.py
"""Hide rules at the pixel level, from the stage-0 copy of Ferrari's warm boulder (V19):

1. planes: a big lit plane is ONE gradient between two adjacent steps, rendered with a 2x2 Bayer
   (DPaint) fill; the form turns only in narrow edge bands. So the torso's interior step
   coordinate is pulled toward its median (flattened), and the silhouette keeps the full turn.
2. edge bands: on silhouette edges turned away from the sun the lit face falls one step in a 1-px
   band; in the shadow family the outermost pixel takes the reflected (saturated) step 2.
3. lit edges: edges toward the sun take the pure upper step (his crown is pure f); dark hide
   (neck, legs) against grass gets a 1-px rim so it separates without an outline.
4. below ~28 px no dither at all: marks are single pixels and a checker there is noise.
"""
import numpy as np
from scipy import ndimage as ndi

BAYER = np.array([[0, 2], [3, 1]])


def bayer_quant(x, off=(0, 0)):
    H, W = x.shape
    yy, xx = np.mgrid[0:H, 0:W]
    t = (BAYER[(yy + off[0]) % 2, (xx + off[1]) % 2] + 0.5) / 4
    fl = np.floor(x)
    return np.clip(fl + ((x - fl) > t), 0, 5).astype(np.int32)


def screen_light(view):
    """The sun's direction projected on the screen: (right, up)."""
    import render
    L = render.sun_vec(view.sun_az, view.sun_el)
    e = np.radians(view.elev)
    sx = L[0]
    sv = L[2] * np.cos(e) + L[1] * np.sin(e)
    v = np.array([sx, sv])
    return v / (np.linalg.norm(v) + 1e-9)


def apply(R, view, tubes, size_px, flatten=0.5, dark_mats=None):
    on = R.mat >= 0
    x = R.x.copy()
    d_in = ndi.distance_transform_cdt(on, metric="taxicab")
    # 1. flatten the torso's interior toward its median lit value
    ti = [i for i, t in enumerate(tubes) if t.name == "torso"]
    if ti and size_px >= 20:
        tor = (R.part == ti[0]) & on
        interior = tor & (d_in >= 3)
        lit = interior & (x >= 3)
        if lit.sum() > 4:
            xp = np.median(x[lit])
            w = np.clip((d_in - 2) / 3.0, 0, 1) * flatten
            sel = tor & (x >= 3)
            x[sel] = x[sel] + (xp - x[sel]) * w[sel]
    # 2./3. edge bands
    if size_px >= 20:
        # outward normal of the silhouette from the smoothed mask gradient
        m = ndi.gaussian_filter(on.astype(float), 1.0)
        gy, gx = np.gradient(m)
        nx, nv = -gx, gy                      # outward: toward decreasing mask; v is up
        nn = np.hypot(nx, nv) + 1e-9
        nx, nv = nx / nn, nv / nn
        sl = screen_light(view)
        facing = nx * sl[0] + nv * sl[1]
        edge = on & (d_in == 1)
        away = edge & (facing < -0.2)
        tow = edge & (facing > 0.35)
        litf = x >= 3
        x[away & litf] = np.minimum(x[away & litf], np.floor(x[away & litf]) - 0.01)
        x[away & ~litf] = np.maximum(x[away & ~litf], 2.0)
        x[tow & litf] = np.minimum(np.floor(x[tow & litf]) + 1.0, 5.0)
        if dark_mats is not None:
            dm = np.isin(R.mat, dark_mats)
            x[tow & dm & ~litf] = np.maximum(x[tow & dm & ~litf], 3.0)
            x[tow & dm & litf] = np.minimum(x[tow & dm & litf] + 1.0, 5.0)
    R.x = np.clip(x, 0, 5)
    if size_px >= 28:
        R.step = bayer_quant(R.x)
    else:
        R.step = np.clip(np.floor(R.x + 0.5), 0, 5).astype(np.int32)
    return R

Write: elements/deer-elk/src/fringe.py
"""Fur edges: where hide meets hide or hide meets air, the boundary is a fringe, not a contour.

SYNTHESIS: "A mass outline isn't drawn as a line. A textured mass ends in a fringe." Hair flows
down the flanks and down-and-back along the neck, so:

  - boundaries between two hide colours (the elk's cape edge, the rump patch, the belly line)
    interlock in short teeth along the hair direction (1 px wide, 1-2 px long);
  - the bull's mane hangs below the neck line as a row of dark ticks (1-3 px, irregular);
  - a winter coat breaks the back line with an occasional 1-px tuft.
All of it is deterministic in (seed, pixel), so it holds still from frame to frame when the
animal holds still.
"""
import numpy as np
import rig


def _h(y, x, seed):
    v = (np.asarray(y, np.int64) * 73856093) ^ (np.asarray(x, np.int64) * 19349663) ^ (seed * 83492791 + 12345)
    return (v % 10007) / 10007.0


def interlock(R, a, b, size_px, seed=0, down=True):
    """Teeth between hide materials a and b. Vertical boundaries get horizontal teeth 1 px deep
    on alternate 2-row blocks; horizontal boundaries get vertical teeth hanging down."""
    if size_px < 30:
        return R
    m = R.mat
    H, W = m.shape
    out = m.copy()
    ys, xs = np.nonzero(m[:, :-1] == a)
    sel = m[ys, xs + 1] == b
    ys, xs = ys[sel], xs[sel]
    # tooth pattern: blocks of 2 rows alternate sides, with a little randomness
    blk = (ys // 2 + (_h(ys // 2, xs, seed) < 0.3)) % 2 == 0
    out[ys[blk], xs[blk]] = b
    ys, xs = np.nonzero(m[:, :-1] == b)
    sel = m[ys, xs + 1] == a
    ys, xs = ys[sel], xs[sel]
    blk = (ys // 2 + (_h(ys // 2, xs, seed + 1) < 0.3)) % 2 == 0
    out[ys[blk], xs[blk]] = a
    # horizontal boundaries (a above b): b teeth reach up into a on alternate columns, a teeth
    # hang down into b on the others (hair flowing down)
    ys, xs = np.nonzero(m[:-1, :] == a)
    sel = m[ys + 1, xs] == b
    ys, xs = ys[sel], xs[sel]
    alt = (xs + (_h(ys, xs // 2, seed + 2) < 0.25)) % 2 == 0
    out[ys[alt] + 1, xs[alt]] = a
    ys, xs = np.nonzero(m[:-1, :] == b)
    sel = m[ys + 1, xs] == a
    ys, xs = ys[sel], xs[sel]
    alt = (xs + (_h(ys, xs // 2, seed + 3) < 0.25)) % 2 == 0
    out[ys[alt] + 1, xs[alt]] = b
    R.mat = out
    return R


def hang(R, parts, mat, size_px, seed=0, density=0.55, maxlen=3):
    """Ticks hanging below the lower silhouette of `parts` (the bull's mane)."""
    if size_px < 24:
        return R
    m = R.mat
    H, W = m.shape
    onp = np.isin(R.part, parts) & (m >= 0)
    below_empty = np.zeros_like(onp)
    below_empty[:-1] = onp[:-1] & (m[1:] < 0)
    ys, xs = np.nonzero(below_empty)
    L = max(1, min(maxlen, int(round(size_px / 30))))
    for y, x in zip(ys, xs):
        hv = _h(y, x, seed)
        if hv > density:
            continue
        n = 1 + int(hv / density * L)
        for j in range(1, n + 1):
            if y + j < H and m[y + j, x] < 0:
                R.mat[y + j, x] = mat
                R.step[y + j, x] = max(0, R.step[y, x] - (1 if j > 1 else 0))
                R.part[y + j, x] = R.part[y, x]
    return R

"""Scale ladder: one animal from 60 px down to 4 px, side (elev 6) and iso (elev 30)."""
import numpy as np, sys
from PIL import Image
import rig, paint
from render import View
tag = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 'x'
SIZES = [60, 48, 40, 32, 24, 20, 16, 12, 10, 8, 6, 5, 4]
subjects = [(rig.Individual('elk', 'f', 'adult', 'autumn', 1), 'stand', 0.0),
            (rig.Individual('elk', 'm', 'adult', 'autumn', 2), 'walk', 0.3),
            (rig.Individual('elk', 'f', 'adult', 'autumn', 3), 'graze', 0.0),
            (rig.Individual('deer', 'f', 'adult', 'autumn', 4), 'alert', 0.0),
            (rig.Individual('elk', 'f', 'young', 'summer', 5), 'walk', 0.6)]
rows = []
for elev, yaw in ((6, -14), (30, -30)):
    for ind, g, ph in subjects:
        cells = []
        for L in SIZES:
            R, _, k = paint.paint_index(ind, g, ph, View(yaw=yaw, elev=elev, sun_el=45, sun_az=-155, ss=8 if L > 12 else 12), size_px=L)
            cells.append(paint.colour(R, ind, 'morning'))
        rows.append(cells)
Z = 4
tiles = []
for cells in rows:
    hh = max(c.shape[0] for c in cells)
    ww = sum(c.shape[1] + 3 for c in cells)
    t = np.full((hh, ww, 3), 40, np.uint8)
    x = 0
    for c in cells:
        t[hh - c.shape[0]:, x:x + c.shape[1]] = c; x += c.shape[1] + 3
    tiles.append(t)
W = max(t.shape[1] for t in tiles)
out = np.full((sum(t.shape[0] + 3 for t in tiles), W, 3), 40, np.uint8)
y = 0
for t in tiles:
    out[y:y + t.shape[0], :t.shape[1]] = t; y += t.shape[0] + 3
Image.fromarray(out.repeat(Z, 0).repeat(Z, 1)).save(f'../stages/s1/ladder_{tag}.png')
print(out.shape)
EOF

Write: elements/deer-elk/src/micro.py
"""The small painter (under ~14 px of body length): mass + strokes.

At these sizes the splat renderer turns legs, neck and antlers into blobs (ladder_micro1). A
painter at this scale does something else: the body is a mass (coverage of the torso only),
and everything narrow is a stroke, a clean 1-px run through the projected skeleton:
  legs      elbow/stifle -> carpus/hock -> hoof, far pair first and one step darker
  neck      withers-front -> poll, 1 px (2 px on a bull at >= 10 px: the mane)
  head      poll -> nose, 1 px
  antlers   the beam as a 1-px run; tines only >= 10 px; at 5-7 px a single dark pixel
  ears      a 1-px tick (deer) at >= 8 px
The value plan is the far read from the naturalist's guide: dark front half (neck, head, legs),
light back, the one pale pixel of the rump; the torso's top row takes the sun.
"""
import numpy as np
import rig, render
from render import clean_line, view_xf


def _poly(img_mat, img_step, img_part, pts, mat, step, part, H, W, overwrite=True):
    for (x0, y0), (x1, y1) in zip(pts[:-1], pts[1:]):
        for (x, y) in clean_line(x0, y0, x1, y1):
            if 0 <= x < W and 0 <= y < H and (overwrite or img_mat[y, x] < 0):
                img_mat[y, x] = mat
                img_step[y, x] = step
                img_part[y, x] = part


def paint(ind, tubes, sp, view, k, size_px):
    f = view_xf(view)
    names = [t.name for t in tubes]
    torso_like = [i for i, t in enumerate(tubes) if t.name == "torso" or t.name.startswith("shoulder")]
    sub = [tubes[i] for i in torso_like]
    import dataclasses
    R = render.rasterize(sub, ind, dataclasses.replace(view, ss=12), k, pad=4)
    # the whole skeleton's extent may exceed the torso's canvas: re-rasterize on a canvas big
    # enough for all tubes by rasterizing everything once for the frame and the ground shadow
    Rall = render.rasterize(tubes, ind, dataclasses.replace(view, ss=6), k, pad=4)
    H, W = Rall.cov.shape
    ox, oy = Rall.origin
    # torso mass placed into the full canvas
    ox_t, oy_t = R.origin
    dx, dy = int(round(ox - ox_t)), int(round(oy - oy_t))
    mat = np.full((H, W), -1, np.int32)
    step = np.zeros((H, W), np.int32)
    part = np.full((H, W), -1, np.int32)

    def P(p):
        sx, sv, d = f(p)
        return (sx * k + ox, oy - sv * k)

    def depth(p):
        return f(p)[2]

    tor = tubes[names.index("torso")]
    dmid = depth(tor.c.mean(0))
    dark_front = ind.species == "elk" and ind.age != "young"
    legmat = rig.LEG
    # legs: far first
    legs = []
    for L in ("LF", "RF", "LH", "RH"):
        segs = [tubes[names.index(L + s)] for s in ("1", "2", "3", "hf")]
        chain = [segs[0].c[1], segs[0].c[-1], segs[1].c[-1], segs[2].c[-1], segs[3].c[-1]]
        d = depth(np.mean(chain, 0))
        legs.append((d, L, chain))
    legs.sort(key=lambda t: -t[0])
    for d, L, chain in legs:
        far = d > dmid
        pts = [P(p) for p in chain]
        _poly(mat, step, part, pts, legmat, 1 if far else 2, names.index(L + "2"), H, W)
    # torso mass on top
    tm = R.cov >= 0.5
    ys, xs = np.nonzero(tm)
    ys2, xs2 = ys + dy, xs + dx
    ok = (ys2 >= 0) & (ys2 < H) & (xs2 >= 0) & (xs2 < W)
    mat[ys2[ok], xs2[ok]] = R.mat[ys[ok], xs[ok]]
    part[ys2[ok], xs2[ok]] = names.index("torso")
    # values: top row of the mass in the sun, the rest one down, the bottom row in shade
    body = np.zeros((H, W), bool)
    body[ys2[ok], xs2[ok]] = True
    top = body & ~np.vstack([np.zeros((1, W), bool), body[:-1]])
    bot = body & ~np.vstack([body[1:], np.zeros((1, W), bool)])
    step[body] = 3
    step[top] = 4
    if size_px >= 8:
        step[bot & ~top] = 2
    # the far read: at very small sizes the torso keeps two colours only, body and rump
    if size_px < 9:
        m = mat[body]
        m[(m != rig.PALE)] = rig.BODY if not dark_front else m[(m != rig.PALE)]
        m[(m == rig.BELLY)] = rig.BODY
        mat[body] = m
        if dark_front:
            # the cape: the front third of the mass is dark
            cols = np.nonzero(body.any(0))[0]
            x0, x1 = cols.min(), cols.max()
            facing_right = P(tor.c[-1])[0] > P(tor.c[0])[0]
            span = x1 - x0 + 1
            nd = max(1, int(round(span * 0.28)))
            front = np.arange(x1 - nd + 1, x1 + 1) if facing_right else np.arange(x0, x0 + nd)
            for c in front:
                sel = body[:, c]
                mat[sel, c] = np.where(mat[sel, c] == rig.PALE, rig.PALE, rig.DARK)
        # rump: guarantee one pale pixel at the rear top of the mass for elk
        if ind.species == "elk" and ind.age != "young" and not (mat == rig.PALE).any():
            cols = np.nonzero(body.any(0))[0]
            facing_right = P(tor.c[-1])[0] > P(tor.c[0])[0]
            c = cols.min() if facing_right else cols.max()
            rr = np.nonzero(body[:, c])[0]
            if len(rr):
                mat[rr[len(rr) // 2], c] = rig.PALE
    # neck and head strokes
    neck = tubes[names.index("neck")]
    head = tubes[names.index("head")]
    nmat = rig.DARK if dark_front else rig.BODY
    npts = [P(neck.c[1]), P(neck.c[-1])]
    _poly(mat, step, part, npts, nmat, 2 if dark_front else 3, names.index("neck"), H, W)
    if ind.species == "elk" and ind.sex == "m" and ind.age == "adult" and size_px >= 10:
        # the mane: a second run one pixel below
        npts2 = [(x, y + 1) for x, y in npts]
        _poly(mat, step, part, npts2, rig.DARK, 1, names.index("neck"), H, W, overwrite=False)
    hpts = [P(head.c[0]), P(head.c[-1])]
    _poly(mat, step, part, hpts, nmat, 2 if dark_front else 3, names.index("head"), H, W)
    # antlers
    ant = ind.antler()
    if ant:
        beams = [i for i, t in enumerate(tubes) if t.name.startswith(("beam", "ant"))]
        if size_px >= 8 or ind.species == "elk":
            for i in beams[:1] if size_px < 10 else beams:
                t = tubes[i]
                pts = [P(t.c[j]) for j in range(0, len(t.c), max(1, len(t.c) // 3))] + [P(t.c[-1])]
                if size_px < 8:
                    pts = [P(t.c[0]), P(t.c[len(t.c) // 2])]
                _poly(mat, step, part, pts, rig.ANTLER if ant != "velvet" else rig.VELVET, 1, i, H, W, overwrite=False)
            if size_px >= 12:
                for i, t in enumerate(tubes):
                    if t.name.startswith("tine") and t.name[4] in "1" and t.name[-1] in "24":
                        _poly(mat, step, part, [P(t.c[0]), P(t.c[-1])], rig.ANTLER, 1, i, H, W, overwrite=False)
    # ears for deer
    if ind.species == "deer" and size_px >= 8:
        for i, t in enumerate(tubes):
            if t.name.startswith("ear") and depth(t.c.mean(0)) < depth(head.c.mean(0)) + 0.05:
                _poly(mat, step, part, [P(t.c[0]), P(t.c[-1])], rig.BODY, 3, i, H, W, overwrite=False)
    # flagged tail (deer)
    tail = tubes[names.index("tail")]
    if ind.species == "deer" and size_px >= 8 and tail.c[-1][2] > tail.c[0][2]:
        _poly(mat, step, part, [P(tail.c[0]), P(tail.c[-1])], rig.PALE, 4, names.index("tail"), H, W, overwrite=False)
    Rall.mat = mat
    Rall.step = step
    Rall.part = part
    Rall.cov = (mat >= 0).astype(float)
    Rall.x = step.astype(float)
    return Rall

44. elements/deer-elk/stages/s1/ladder_micro2.png

Write: elements/deer-elk/src/micro.py
"""The small painter (under ~14 px of body length): a designed glyph from the projected skeleton.

At these sizes the splat renderer turns legs, neck and antlers into blobs (stages/s1/
ladder_micro1). A pixel artist at this scale places pixels, so this painter does too, from
keypoints of the same rig:

  body   per column, the torso's projected top and bottom, rounded; at least 2 rows (>= 6 px)
         and never more rows than it has columns / 2.5; corners knocked off at >= 9 px
  zones  elk: the rear ~18% of the body is the pale rump, the front ~28% the dark cape;
         deer and calves: one colour. Top row in the sun, the rest one step down.
  legs   per pair (fore, hind), the near leg as a clean 1-px run from the belly to the hoof;
         the far leg only when its hoof is 2+ px away from the near one (walking, running),
         one step darker
  neck   base -> poll, 1 px (a bull's mane makes it 2 at >= 10 px)
  head   poll -> nose, 1 px; below 7 px just the poll and the nose
  antler bull elk: the beam as a 1-px run rising back from the poll (>= 8 px); a single dark
         pixel above the head below that; tines at >= 12 px
  ears   deer: a 1-px tick up from the poll at >= 8 px
The value plan is the far read from the naturalist's guide: dark front, light back, the pale
rump pixel, the head up or down.
"""
import numpy as np
import dataclasses
import rig, render
from render import clean_line, view_xf


def _put(mat, step, part, x, y, m, s, p, overwrite=True):
    H, W = mat.shape
    if 0 <= x < W and 0 <= y < H and (overwrite or mat[y, x] < 0):
        mat[y, x] = m
        step[y, x] = s
        part[y, x] = p


def _line(mat, step, part, a, b, m, s, p, overwrite=True):
    for (x, y) in clean_line(a[0], a[1], b[0], b[1]):
        _put(mat, step, part, x, y, m, s, p, overwrite)


def paint(ind, tubes, sp, view, k, size_px):
    f = view_xf(view)
    names = [t.name for t in tubes]
    Rall = render.rasterize(tubes, ind, dataclasses.replace(view, ss=8), k, pad=4)
    H, W = Rall.cov.shape
    ox, oy = Rall.origin
    mat = np.full((H, W), -1, np.int32)
    step = np.zeros((H, W), np.int32)
    part = np.full((H, W), -1, np.int32)

    def P(p):
        sx, sv, d = f(np.asarray(p))
        return np.stack([sx * k + ox, oy - sv * k], -1)

    def depth(p):
        return f(np.asarray(p))[2]

    tor = tubes[names.index("torso")]
    ti = names.index("torso")
    elk_adult = ind.species == "elk" and ind.age != "young"
    # --- torso silhouette per column from projected surface rings
    th = np.linspace(0, 2 * np.pi, 24, endpoint=False)
    T, N1, N2 = render._frames(tor.c, tor.up)
    ring = tor.c[:, None, :] + tor.a[:, None, None] * np.cos(th)[None, :, None] * N1[:, None, :] + tor.b[:, None, None] * np.sin(th)[None, :, None] * N2[:, None, :]
    pr = P(ring.reshape(-1, 3))
    xs, ys = pr[:, 0], pr[:, 1]
    x0, x1 = int(np.floor(xs.min() + 0.5)), int(np.floor(xs.max() - 0.5))
    ncol = max(x1 - x0 + 1, 2)
    minrows = 2 if size_px >= 6 else 1
    front_right = P(tor.c[-1])[0] > P(tor.c[0])[0]
    cols = {}
    for cx in range(x0, x0 + ncol):
        sel = np.abs(xs - (cx + 0.5)) < 0.75
        if not sel.any():
            continue
        top, bot = ys[sel].min(), ys[sel].max()
        t, b = int(round(top)), int(round(bot))
        if b - t < minrows:
            mid = (top + bot) / 2
            t = int(round(mid - minrows / 2))
            b = t + minrows
        cols[cx] = (t, b)
    maxrows = max(minrows, int(round(ncol / 2.4)))
    for cx, (t, b) in list(cols.items()):
        if b - t > maxrows:
            cols[cx] = (b - maxrows, b)
    # knock off corners at >= 9 px: first and last column lose their top row
    cks = sorted(cols)
    if size_px >= 9 and len(cks) > 4:
        for cx in (cks[0], cks[-1]):
            t, b = cols[cx]
            if b - t > 1:
                cols[cx] = (t + 1, b)
    # zones along the body
    span = cks[-1] - cks[0] + 1
    for cx in cks:
        t, b = cols[cx]
        u = (cx - cks[0]) / max(span - 1, 1)
        if not front_right:
            u = 1 - u
        for y in range(t, b):
            m = rig.BODY
            if elk_adult:
                if u < 0.18:
                    m = rig.PALE if y < b - 1 or b - t < 3 else rig.DARK
                elif u > 0.72:
                    m = rig.DARK
            s = 4 if y == t else 3
            if y == b - 1 and b - t >= 3:
                s = 2
            if m == rig.DARK:
                s = min(s, 3)
            _put(mat, step, part, cx, y, m, s, ti)
    body = mat >= 0

    def belly_y(x):
        x = int(round(x))
        if x in cols:
            return cols[x][1] - 1
        near = min(cols, key=lambda c: abs(c - x))
        return cols[near][1] - 1

    # --- legs
    pairs = {"F": [], "H": []}
    for L in ("LF", "RF", "LH", "RH"):
        segs = [tubes[names.index(L + s)] for s in ("1", "2", "3", "hf")]
        chain = np.array([segs[0].c[-1] * 0.5 + segs[0].c[1] * 0.5, segs[0].c[-1], segs[1].c[-1], segs[2].c[-1], segs[3].c[-1]])
        pairs[L[1]].append((depth(chain.mean(0)), L, P(chain)))
    legmat = rig.LEG if (elk_adult or ind.species == "elk") else rig.LEG
    for key in ("F", "H"):
        pr_ = sorted(pairs[key], key=lambda t: t[0])       # near first
        near, far = pr_[0], pr_[1]
        drawn = [near]
        if abs(near[2][-1][0] - far[2][-1][0]) >= 1.6 and size_px >= 6:
            drawn.append(far)
        for j, (d, L, pts) in enumerate(drawn[::-1]):
            is_far = (L == far[1]) and len(drawn) == 2
            p0 = pts[0].copy()
            p0[1] = belly_y(p0[0])
            chain = [p0] + ([pts[1], pts[2]] if size_px >= 9 else []) + [pts[-1]]
            s = 1 if is_far else 2
            for a, b in zip(chain[:-1], chain[1:]):
                _line(mat, step, part, a, b, legmat, s, names.index(L + "2"), overwrite=False)
    # --- neck and head
    neck = tubes[names.index("neck")]
    head = tubes[names.index("head")]
    nmat = rig.DARK if elk_adult else rig.BODY
    ns = 2 if elk_adult else 3
    nb = P(neck.c[1])
    # start the neck at the body's top-front edge
    poll = P(neck.c[-1])
    nose = P(head.c[-1])
    _line(mat, step, part, nb, poll, nmat, ns, names.index("neck"), overwrite=False)
    if ind.species == "elk" and ind.sex == "m" and ind.age == "adult" and size_px >= 10:
        _line(mat, step, part, nb + [0, 1], poll + [0, 1], rig.DARK, 1, names.index("neck"), overwrite=False)
    if size_px >= 7:
        _line(mat, step, part, poll, nose, nmat, ns, names.index("head"))
    else:
        _put(mat, step, part, int(round(nose[0])), int(round(nose[1])), nmat, ns, names.index("head"), overwrite=False)
    # --- antlers
    ant = ind.antler()
    if ant:
        am = rig.ANTLER if ant != "velvet" else rig.VELVET
        beams = [t for t in tubes if t.name.startswith(("beam", "ant"))]
        beams.sort(key=lambda t: depth(t.c.mean(0)))
        if size_px >= 8:
            for bt in beams[:1] if size_px < 11 else beams[:2]:
                pts = P(bt.c[[0, len(bt.c) // 2, -1]])
                _line(mat, step, part, pts[0], pts[1], am, 1, ti, overwrite=False)
                _line(mat, step, part, pts[1], pts[2], am, 1, ti, overwrite=False)
            if size_px >= 12:
                for t in tubes:
                    if t.name.startswith("tine") and t.name[-1] in "02" and depth(t.c.mean(0)) < depth(head.c.mean(0)):
                        pt = P(t.c[[0, -1]])
                        _line(mat, step, part, pt[0], pt[1], am, 1, ti, overwrite=False)
        elif ind.species == "elk":
            top = P(beams[0].c[len(beams[0].c) // 2])
            _put(mat, step, part, int(round(top[0])), int(round(top[1])), am, 1, ti, overwrite=False)
    # --- ears (deer)
    if ind.species == "deer" and size_px >= 8:
        ears = [t for t in tubes if t.name.startswith("ear")]
        e = min(ears, key=lambda t: depth(t.c.mean(0)))
        pe = P(e.c[[0, -1]])
        _line(mat, step, part, pe[0], pe[1], rig.BODY, 3, names.index(e.name), overwrite=False)
    # flagged tail
    tail = tubes[names.index("tail")]
    if ind.species == "deer" and size_px >= 8 and tail.c[-1][2] > tail.c[0][2]:
        pt = P(tail.c[[0, -1]])
        _line(mat, step, part, pt[0], pt[1], rig.PALE, 4, names.index("tail"), overwrite=False)
    Rall.mat = mat
    Rall.step = step
    Rall.part = part
    Rall.cov = (mat >= 0).astype(float)
    Rall.x = step.astype(float)
    return Rall

"""Pose sheet: every gait and phase for elk cow, bull and deer at one size (grey form check + colour)."""
import numpy as np, sys
from PIL import Image
import rig, paint
from render import View
tag = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 'x'
size = int(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 48
G = [('stand', 0), ('graze', 0), ('alert', 0), ('bugle', 0), ('lie', 0)] + [('walk', p) for p in (0, .25, .5, .75)] + \
    [('trot', p) for p in (0, .25, .5, .75)] + [('gallop', p) for p in (0, .2, .4, .6, .8)] + [('bound', p) for p in (0, .3, .6)] + [('stot', p) for p in (0, .25)]
inds = [rig.Individual('elk', 'f', 'adult', 'autumn', 1), rig.Individual('elk', 'm', 'adult', 'autumn', 2), rig.Individual('deer', 'f', 'adult', 'autumn', 3)]
rows = []
for ind in inds:
    cells = []
    for g, ph in G:
        R, _, k = paint.paint_index(ind, g, ph, View(yaw=-14, elev=6, sun_el=45, sun_az=-155), size_px=size)
        cells.append(paint.colour(R, ind, 'morning'))
    rows.append(cells)
Z = 3
tiles = []
for cells in rows:
    hh = max(c.shape[0] for c in cells)
    t = np.full((hh, sum(c.shape[1] + 3 for c in cells), 3), 40, np.uint8)
    x = 0
    for c in cells:
        t[hh - c.shape[0]:, x:x + c.shape[1]] = c; x += c.shape[1] + 3
    tiles.append(t)
W = max(t.shape[1] for t in tiles)
out = np.full((sum(t.shape[0] + 3 for t in tiles), W, 3), 40, np.uint8)
y = 0
for t in tiles:
    out[y:y + t.shape[0], :t.shape[1]] = t; y += t.shape[0] + 3
Image.fromarray(out.repeat(Z, 0).repeat(Z, 1)).save(f'../stages/s1/poses_{tag}.png')
print(out.shape)
EOF

cat > one.py <<'EOF'
import numpy as np, sys
from PIL import Image
import rig, paint
from render import View
spn,sex,age,g,ph,size,yaw,elev=sys.argv[1],sys.argv[2],sys.argv[3],sys.argv[4],float(sys.argv[5]),int(sys.argv[6]),float(sys.argv[7]),float(sys.argv[8])

# Grass at every distance: a study of the handoff

*Grass that zooms continuously from a person 30 px tall (30 px/m) to a whole region
(0.3 px/m) without popping, in Mark Ferrari's painted-pixel language.*

Everything here is made by `src/` (package `grasslod/`).  `src/render_all.sh` regenerates
every image.  Paths below are relative to this folder.

**Deliverables**

- `img/zoom_golden.gif`, `img/zoom_day.gif`: 96-frame continuous zo

"""Labelled parameter sheets.   nice -n 10 python3 sheets.py [A B C D E F]

A  hour x distance        B  species x distance     C  moisture x grazing
D  wind                   E  camera angle x distance F  seeds
Each cell is the same machinery (grasslod.paint) with one or two parameters changed.
"""
import os, sys
os.environ.setdefault("OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS", "1")
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw
from multiprocessing import Pool

OUT = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "sheets")
CW, CH, SC = 240, 150, 2


def cell(args):
    wkw, ckw, hour, mkw = args
    from grasslod.world import World
    from grasslod.render import Camera
    from grasslod.painter import paint, centre_on_bank
    from grasslod import palette as P
    w = World(**wkw)
    cy, cz = centre_on_bank(w)
    cam = Camera(W=CW, H=CH, cx=0, cy=cy, cz=cz, **ckw)
    idx, pal = paint(w, cam, hour, mparams=mkw)
    return P.to_rgb(idx, pal)


def sheet(name, rows, cols, make, title):
    """make(r, c) -> (world kw, camera kw, hour, mark kw)"""
    jobs = [make(r, c) for r in rows for c in cols]
    with Pool(4) as p:
        tiles = p.map(cell, jobs)
    lw, th = 110, 26
    W = lw + len(cols) * (CW * SC + 6)
    H = th + 18 + len(rows) * (CH * SC + 6)
    im = Image.new("RGB", (W, H), (24, 24, 28))
    d = ImageDraw.Draw(im)
    d.text((8, 6), title, fill=(235, 235, 235))

@dataclass
class World:
    seed: int = 1
    species: str = "meadow"
    moisture: float = 0.5      # 0 dry .. 1 wet  (shifts hue green<->straw, sedge in hollows)
    grazing: float = 0.2       # 0 ungrazed .. 1 close-cropped
    wind: float = 0.3          # 0 calm .. 1 strong (lean + combing)
    wind_dir: float = 1.0      # +1 blows to screen right, -1 to the left
    season_dead: float = 0.05  # base fraction of straw (dead) blades
    relief: float = 1.0        # scales regional hills
    hummock: float = 0.3       # amplitude (m) of the 2-6 m hummocks
    water_level: float = 0.0

    # ----- geometry --------------------------------------------------------------
    def channel(self, x):
        """Stream centre line y_c(x) and half width."""
        s = self.seed
        yc = (5.0 * np.sin(x / 37.0 + s) + 2.0 * np.sin(x / 11.0 + 2 * s)
              + 60.0 * np.sin(x / 420.0 + 0.3 * s))
        return yc, 1.6

def paint(world: World, cam: Camera, hour="day", vparams=None, mparams=None, seed=0,
          marks=True, return_layers=False):
    pal, sun = P.build(hour)
    R = Raster(world, cam)
    R.sun_screen_x = sun[0]
    V, lit, lam, graze = value_design(R, sun, vparams)
    H, W = V.shape
    mp = MarkParams(**(mparams or {}))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    # base layer: design value, stochastic dither between adjacent steps
    base = V.copy()
    canvas = np.full((H, W), np.nan)
    cmat = np.zeros((H, W), np.int8)
    prio = np.full((H, W), -1, np.int64)
    ppm, s, c = cam.ppm, cam.s, cam.c
    fin = np.isfinite(R.depth)
    grass = (R.mat == 1)
    bgv = V.copy()
    bgv = water_background(R, V, bgv)   # water: light sky tint, or the dark mirrored bank
    bgv[R.sky] = 5.0

Write: elements/deer-elk/src/ground.py
"""A calm meadow to stand the animals on (not the subject of this study; grass-lod owns grass).

Ferrari's middle-distance grass is a checker of 2-3 close greens with the form turned by the
density of the lighter one (SYNTHESIS, grass-lod stage 0). So: value noise at a few metres
decides the local density of the lighter step; a 2x2 Bayer turns density into pixels; near
the bottom of a landscape view, sparse vertical 1x2 ticks of the darker step. Everything in
(material GROUND, step) so the hour palette lights it.
"""
import numpy as np
from scipy import ndimage as ndi

BAYER = np.array([[0, 2], [3, 1]])


def value_noise(shape, cell, rng):
    h, w = shape
    gh, gw = h // cell + 3, w // cell + 3
    g = rng.random((gh, gw))
    return ndi.zoom(g, cell, order=3)[:h, :w]


def meadow(shape, k, seed=0, elev=8.0, base=3.0, contrast=0.9, ticks=True):
    """Returns an int step array (0..5) for a meadow of `shape`, k px per metre."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    h, w = shape
    cell = int(np.clip(k * 3.0, 3, 40))
    n = value_noise(shape, cell, rng) * 0.65 + value_noise(shape, max(3, cell // 3), rng) * 0.35
    x = base - 0.5 + (n - 0.5) * 2 * contrast + 0.5
    yy, xx = np.mgrid[0:h, 0:w]
    t = (BAYER[yy % 2, xx % 2] + 0.5) / 4
    fl = np.floor(x)
    st = (fl + ((x - fl) > t)).astype(np.int32)
    if ticks and k >= 6:
        # near grass: sparse vertical ticks of the darker step (minority marks in a lit mass)
        dens = 0.05 * np.clip(k / 20, 0.3, 1.5)
        m = rng.random(shape) < dens
        L = max(1, int(round(k / 14)))
        for j in range(L + 1):
            mm = np.roll(m, j, axis=0)
            st[mm] = np.minimum(st[mm], np.floor(x[mm]).astype(np.int32) - 1)
    return np.clip(st, 0, 5)

Write: elements/deer-elk/src/herd.py
"""Herds: who stands where, facing which way, doing what; and a compositor that paints them on a
meadow at any scale and camera elevation (landscape ~8 deg, iso 30 deg, air 60 deg).

Arrangements from the naturalist's guide (drawing-animals.md s.5, elk-and-deer.md A1):
  graze    loose, 3-10 m apart, all roughly facing one way (into the wind / across the slope),
           calves at a cow's flank, 1-2 sentinels head-up; composition ~19 cows : 8 calves : 3
           yearlings per 30
  alert    the scatter collapses into a knot 2-4 m apart, every head up, all facing the threat
  file     single file on a trail, 3-6 m apart, lead cow in front, calves in the middle,
           yearlings dawdling at the back
  flee     the file at a trot (elk's travelling gait), tails of the file still walking
  bedded   2-5 m apart, legs folded, heads in different directions
  rut      a bull bugling among his cows, a satellite bull 50-100 m off
  deer     a doe with twins walking in line; a buck alone
"""
import numpy as np
from dataclasses import dataclass
import rig


@dataclass
class Member:
    ind: rig.Individual
    gait: str
    phase: float
    x: float          # metres, world (x right, y away from the viewer)
    y: float
    heading: float    # degrees in the world: 0 = +x (screen right), 90 = away


def _poisson(n, r_min, r_max, rng, spread=1.0, centre=(0, 0)):
    pts = [np.array(centre, float)]
    tries = 0
    while len(pts) < n and tries < 20000:
        tries += 1
        base = pts[rng.integers(len(pts))]
        a = rng.random() * 2 * np.pi
        d = r_min + rng.random() * (r_max - r_min)
        p = base + d * np.array([np.cos(a), np.sin(a) * spread])
        if all(np.linalg.norm(p - q) >= r_min for q in pts):
            pts.append(p)
    return pts


def composition(n, rng, rut=False):
    """-> list of (sex, age) for a Roosevelt cow-calf herd of n."""
    out = [("f", "adult")]                        # the lead cow first
    for i in range(1, n):
        r = rng.random()
        if r < 0.27:
            out.append(("f", "young"))
        elif r < 0.37:
            out.append(("m" if rng.random() < 0.5 else "f", "yearling"))
        else:
            out.append(("f", "adult"))
    if rut:
        out[1] = ("m", "adult")
    return out


def elk_herd(behaviour="graze", n=20, seed=0, heading=10.0, season="autumn", trail=None):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    comp = composition(n, rng, rut=behaviour == "rut")
    mem = []
    if behaviour in ("graze", "rut"):
        pts = _poisson(n, 3.0, 8.0, rng, spread=0.7)
        adults = []
        for i, ((sex, age), p) in enumerate(zip(comp, pts)):
            h = heading + rng.normal(0, 30)
            if age == "young" and adults:
                # calves at a cow's flank
                m = adults[rng.integers(len(adults))]
                side = rng.choice([-1, 1])
                hh = np.radians(m.heading)
                p = np.array([m.x, m.y]) + 1.4 * side * np.array([-np.sin(hh), np.cos(hh)]) + rng.normal(0, 0.4, 2)
                h = m.heading + rng.normal(0, 20)
            r = rng.random()
            if behaviour == "rut" and sex == "m" and age == "adult":
                g, ph = "bugle", 0.0
            elif age == "young":
                g, ph = ("walk", rng.random()) if r < 0.3 else (("lie", 0.0) if r < 0.45 else ("graze", 0.0))
            elif r < 0.1:
                g, ph = "alert", 0.0              # sentinels
            elif r < 0.25:
                g, ph = "walk", rng.random()
            else:
                g, ph = "graze", 0.0
            m = Member(rig.Individual("elk", sex, age, season, seed * 100 + i), g, ph, float(p[0]), float(p[1]), float(h))
            mem.append(m)
            if age == "adult" and sex == "f":
                adults.append(m)
        if behaviour == "rut":
            b = Member(rig.Individual("elk", "m", "adult", season, seed * 100 + 99), "alert", 0.0,
                       float(pts[0][0] + 55), float(pts[0][1] + 25), heading + 180)
            mem.append(b)
    elif behaviour == "alert":
        pts = _poisson(n, 2.0, 3.5, rng, spread=0.8)
        for i, ((sex, age), p) in enumerate(zip(comp, pts)):
            h = heading + rng.normal(0, 12)
            mem.append(Member(rig.Individual("elk", sex, age, season, seed * 100 + i), "alert", 0.0, float(p[0]), float(p[1]), float(h)))
        # calves to the middle
        c = np.mean([[m.x, m.y] for m in mem], 0)
        for m in mem:
            if m.ind.age == "young":
                m.x, m.y = (m.x - c[0]) * 0.4 + c[0], (m.y - c[1]) * 0.4 + c[1]
    elif behaviour in ("file", "flee"):
        # a trail: a gentle S across the meadow
        order = [comp[0]] + [c for c in comp[1:] if c[1] == "adult"][: n // 3] + [c for c in comp[1:] if c[1] == "young"] + \
                [c for c in comp[1:] if c[1] == "adult"][n // 3:] + [c for c in comp[1:] if c[1] == "yearling"]
        s = 0.0
        hd = np.radians(heading)
        for i, (sex, age) in enumerate(order):
            s -= rng.uniform(3.0, 6.0) * (1.6 if age == "yearling" and rng.random() < 0.5 else 1.0)
            # position along the trail
            x = s * np.cos(hd) + 4.0 * np.sin(s / 14.0) * -np.sin(hd)
            y = s * np.sin(hd) + 4.0 * np.sin(s / 14.0) * np.cos(hd)
            # local heading = trail tangent
            dx = np.cos(hd) - 4.0 / 14.0 * np.cos(s / 14.0) * np.sin(hd)
            dy = np.sin(hd) + 4.0 / 14.0 * np.cos(s / 14.0) * np.cos(hd)
            h = np.degrees(np.arctan2(dy, dx))
            if behaviour == "flee":
                g = "trot" if i < len(order) - 3 else "gallop"
            else:
                g = "walk"
            mem.append(Member(rig.Individual("elk", sex, age, season, seed * 100 + i), g, rng.random(), float(x), float(y), float(h)))
    elif behaviour == "bedded":
        pts = _poisson(n, 2.5, 5.0, rng, spread=0.8)
        for i, ((sex, age), p) in enumerate(zip(comp, pts)):
            g = "lie" if rng.random() < 0.8 else ("alert" if rng.random() < 0.5 else "stand")
            mem.append(Member(rig.Individual("elk", sex, age, season, seed * 100 + i), g, 0.0, float(p[0]), float(p[1]), float(rng.uniform(0, 360))))
    return mem


def deer_group(kind="doe_twins", seed=0, heading=0.0, season="autumn"):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    mem = []
    if kind == "doe_twins":
        hd = np.radians(heading)
        for i, age in enumerate(["adult", "young", "young"]):
            d = -i * rng.uniform(1.6, 2.4)
            mem.append(Member(rig.Individual("deer", "f", age, "summer", seed * 10 + i), "walk", rng.random(),
                              d * np.cos(hd) + rng.normal(0, 0.3), d * np.sin(hd) + rng.normal(0, 0.3), heading + rng.normal(0, 10)))
    elif kind == "buck":
        mem.append(Member(rig.Individual("deer", "m", "adult", season, seed), "alert", 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, heading))
    elif kind == "flee":
        for i in range(3):
            mem.append(Member(rig.Individual("deer", "f" if i else "m", "adult", season, seed * 10 + i), "bound", rng.random(),
                              -i * 3.0 + rng.normal(0, 0.5), rng.normal(0, 1.5), heading + rng.normal(0, 8)))
    return mem


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- compositor

def render_member(args):
    m, k, elev, sun_az, sun_el, hour, depth, ground = args
    import paint, palette as P
    from render import View
    # the individual is painted at its own size in px: body length = 1.62 H k
    size = 1.62 * m.ind.spec().H * k
    R, _, _ = paint.paint_index(m.ind, m.gait, m.phase, View(yaw=m.heading, elev=elev, sun_az=sun_az - 0.0, sun_el=sun_el), size_px=size)
    tab = P.palette(m.ind, hour, depth, ground)
    on = R.mat >= 0
    rgb = np.zeros(R.mat.shape + (3,), np.uint8)
    rgb[on] = tab[R.mat[on], R.step[on]]
    return rgb, on, R.ground_shadow > 0.45, R.origin


def scene(members, k, W, H, elev=30.0, hour="morning", sun_az=-150.0, sun_el=None, ground="summer", depth=0.0,
          centre=None, seed=0, pool=None):
    """Paint the members on a meadow W x H px at k px/m. Returns RGB."""
    import palette as P, ground as G
    hr = P.HOURS[hour]
    sun_el = sun_el if sun_el is not None else hr["el"]
    e = np.radians(elev)
    if centre is None:
        xs = [m.x for m in members]; ys = [m.y for m in members]
        centre = ((min(xs) + max(xs)) / 2, (min(ys) + max(ys)) / 2)
    tab = P.palette(rig.Individual(), hour, depth, ground)
    g = G.meadow((H, W), k, seed=seed, elev=elev)
    img = tab[P.GROUND][g]
    gsh = tab[P.GSHADOW][np.clip(g - 1, 0, 5)]
    order = sorted(members, key=lambda m: -m.y)
    jobs = [(m, k, elev, sun_az, sun_el, hour, depth, ground) for m in order]
    res = pool.map(render_member, jobs) if pool else [render_member(j) for j in jobs]

    def place(m):
        sx = (m.x - centre[0]) * k + W / 2
        sy = H / 2 - (m.y - centre[1]) * np.sin(e) * k
        return sx, sy
    # shadows first (all of them), then the bodies far to near
    for m, (rgb, on, sh, (ox, oy)) in zip(order, res):
        sx, sy = place(m)
        x0, y0 = int(round(sx - ox)), int(round(sy - oy))
        hh, ww = on.shape
        ys, xs = np.nonzero(sh)
        ys2, xs2 = ys + y0, xs + x0
        ok = (ys2 >= 0) & (ys2 < H) & (xs2 >= 0) & (xs2 < W)
        img[ys2[ok], xs2[ok]] = gsh[ys2[ok], xs2[ok]]
    for m, (rgb, on, sh, (ox, oy)) in zip(order, res):
        sx, sy = place(m)
        x0, y0 = int(round(sx - ox)), int(round(sy - oy))
        ys, xs = np.nonzero(on)
        ys2, xs2 = ys + y0, xs + x0
        ok = (ys2 >= 0) & (ys2 < H) & (xs2 >= 0) & (xs2 < W)
        img[ys2[ok], xs2[ok]] = rgb[ys[ok], xs[ok]]
    return img

"""Labelled parameter sheets.     nice -n 10 python3 sheets.py [A B C D E F G H]

A  individual (species x sex x age) x pose            48 px, landscape view
B  individual x size (60 .. 4 px)                     landscape and iso
C  individual x facing (12 yaws)                      40 px, landscape and iso
D  individual x hour (dawn .. overcast)               48 px
E  seeds: individual variation                        40 px
F  gait cycles: walk / trot / gallop / bound in 8 phases, elk and deer
G  herds x distance (k px/m) x camera (landscape 8 deg, iso 30 deg)
H  seasons: coat and antler state through the year
Every cell is one call of paint.paint_index (or herd.scene) with parameters changed.
"""
import os, sys
os.environ.setdefault("OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS", "1")
os.environ.setdefault("OMP_NUM_THREADS", "1")
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont
from multiprocessing import Pool

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
OUT = os.path.join(HERE, "..", "sheets")

SUBJ = {
    "elk bull": ("elk", "m", "adult"), "elk cow": ("elk", "f", "adult"), "elk yearling": ("elk", "m", "yearling"),
    "elk calf": ("elk", "f", "young"), "deer buck": ("deer", "m", "adult"), "deer doe": ("deer", "f", "adult"),
    "deer fawn": ("deer", "f", "young"),
}
HOUR_SUN = {"dawn": (-165, 8), "morning": (-150, 30), "noon": (-110, 60), "golden": (-160, 14), "dusk": (-170, 5), "overcast": (-130, 55)}


def _font(sz=12):
    for p in ["/System/Library/Fonts/Supplemental/Arial.ttf", "/System/Library/Fonts/Helvetica.ttc", "/Library/Fonts/Arial.ttf"]:
        try:
            return ImageFont.truetype(p, sz)
        except Exception:
            pass
    return ImageFont.load_default()


def cell(args):
    import rig, paint
    from render import View
    subj, gait, phase, size, yaw, elev, hour, season, seed = args
    spn, sex, age = SUBJ[subj]
    ind = rig.Individual(spn, sex, age, season, seed)
    az, el = HOUR_SUN[hour]
    R, _, k = paint.paint_index(ind, gait, phase, View(yaw=yaw, elev=elev, sun_az=az, sun_el=el), size_px=size)
    ground = {"winter": "winter", "autumn": "autumn", "spring": "spring"}.get(season, "summer")
    return paint.colour(R, ind, hour, ground=ground, rng=np.random.default_rng(seed + 7))


def grid(name, title, rows, cols, make, zoom=3, pool=None, cellpad=6, note=""):
    jobs = [make(r, c) for r in rows for c in cols]
    tiles = pool.map(cell, jobs) if pool else [cell(j) for j in jobs]
    tiles = [tiles[i * len(cols):(i + 1) * len(cols)] for i in range(len(rows))]
    cw = [max(tiles[i][j].shape[1] for i in range(len(rows))) for j in range(len(cols))]
    rh = [max(t.shape[0] for t in tiles[i]) for i in range(len(rows))]
    lw, th = 118, 44
    f, fs = _font(13), _font(11)
    W = lw + sum(c * zoom + cellpad for c in cw) + 10
    H = th + 20 + sum(r * zoom + cellpad for r in rh) + (18 if note else 0)
    im = Image.new("RGB", (W, H), (24, 24, 28))
    d = ImageDraw.Draw(im)
    d.text((8, 6), title, fill=(236, 236, 236), font=f)
    x = lw
    for j, c in enumerate(cols):
        d.text((x + 2, th), str(c), fill=(190, 190, 200), font=fs)
        x += cw[j] * zoom + cellpad
    y = th + 20
    for i, r in enumerate(rows):
        d.text((8, y + rh[i] * zoom // 2 - 6), str(r), fill=(190, 190, 200), font=fs)
        x = lw
        for j in range(len(cols)):
            t = tiles[i][j]
            t = t.repeat(zoom, 0).repeat(zoom, 1)
            im.paste(Image.fromarray(t), (x, y + rh[i] * zoom - t.shape[0]))
            x += cw[j] * zoom + cellpad
        y += rh[i] * zoom + cellpad
    if note:
        d.text((8, H - 16), note, fill=(160, 160, 170), font=fs)
    os.makedirs(OUT, exist_ok=True)
    im.save(os.path.join(OUT, name))
    print(name, im.size)


POSES = [("stand", 0), ("graze", 0), ("walk", 0.25), ("trot", 0.25), ("gallop", 0.4), ("alert", 0), ("lie", 0)]


def sheet_A(pool):
    cols = ["stand", "graze", "walk", "trot", "gallop", "alert", "lie", "bugle / bound", "stot"]
    rows = list(SUBJ)

    def make(r, c):
        spn = SUBJ[r][0]
        g, ph = {"stand": ("stand", 0), "graze": ("graze", 0), "walk": ("walk", 0.25), "trot": ("trot", 0.25),
                 "gallop": ("gallop", 0.4), "alert": ("alert", 0), "lie": ("lie", 0)}.get(c, (None, 0))
        if c == "bugle / bound":
            g, ph = ("bugle", 0) if (spn == "elk" and r == "elk bull") else (("bound", 0.3) if spn == "deer" else ("walk", 0.6))
        if c == "stot":
            g, ph = ("stot", 0.25) if spn == "deer" else ("trot", 0.75)
        return (r, g, ph, 48, -14, 8, "morning", "autumn", 11)
    grid("A_individual_x_pose.png", "A. individual x pose  (48 px body length, landscape view 8 deg, morning, autumn; elk columns 8-9 show walk/trot where the pose is deer-only)",
         rows, cols, make, zoom=3, pool=pool)


def sheet_B(pool):
    cols = [60, 48, 40, 32, 24, 20, 16, 12, 10, 8, 6, 5, 4]
    rows = [(s, v) for v in ("landscape", "iso 30") for s in ("elk bull", "elk cow", "elk calf", "deer doe")]

    def make(r, c):
        s, v = r
        g = {"elk bull": ("walk", 0.3), "elk cow": ("graze", 0), "elk calf": ("walk", 0.6), "deer doe": ("alert", 0)}[s]
        return (s, g[0], g[1], c, -14 if v == "landscape" else -35, 8 if v == "landscape" else 30, "morning", "autumn", 5)
    grid("B_individual_x_size.png", "B. size ladder: body length in px (>= 14 px: splat renderer + hide rules; < 14 px: the glyph painter)",
         [f"{s} ({v})" for s, v in rows], cols, lambda r, c: make(rows[[f"{s} ({v})" for s, v in rows].index(r)], c), zoom=4, pool=pool)


def sheet_C(pool):
    cols = [0, -30, -60, -90, -120, -150, 180, 150, 120, 90, 60, 30]

    labels = [f"{s} ({v})" for s, v in rows]

    def make(r, c):
        s, v = rows[labels.index(r)]
        g = {"elk bull": ("stand", 0), "elk cow": ("walk", 0.2), "deer buck": ("alert", 0)}[s]
        return (s, g[0], g[1], 40, c, 8 if v == "landscape" else 30, "morning", "autumn", 3)
    grid("C_individual_x_facing.png", "C. facing: yaw in degrees (0 = facing right, -90 = toward the viewer, 90 = away), 40 px",
         labels, cols, make, zoom=3, pool=pool)

    grid("D_individual_x_hour.png", "D. light and hour (48 px; the palette is the light: sun colour, ambient, key and sun direction per hour)",

def sheet_F(pool):

def sheet_G(pool):
    import herd
    panels = []
    specs = [("graze, iso 30, 10 px/m (hero distance ~30 m)", "graze", 10, 30, 360, 220),
             ("graze, landscape 8 deg, 10 px/m", "graze", 10, 8, 360, 140),
             ("graze, iso 30, 4 px/m (~100 m)", "graze", 4, 30, 240, 140),
             ("alert knot, iso 30, 6 px/m", "alert", 6, 30, 240, 140),
             ("moving file, iso 30, 3 px/m (~150 m)", "file", 3, 30, 320, 150),
             ("fleeing file at a trot, landscape 8 deg, 5 px/m", "flee", 5, 8, 320, 110),
             ("bedded, iso 30, 8 px/m", "bedded", 8, 30, 260, 150),
             ("rut: bull bugling, satellite bull off to the right, iso 30, 4 px/m", "rut", 4, 30, 360, 160),
             ("graze, iso 30, 1.6 px/m (~300 m: the far read)", "graze", 1.6, 30, 160, 90),
             ("graze, air 60 deg, 3 px/m", "graze", 3, 60, 200, 140)]
    for lab, beh, k, elev, W, H in specs:
        mem = herd.elk_herd(beh, 26, seed=7, heading=12 if beh not in ("file", "flee") else 6)
        img = herd.scene(mem, k, W, H, elev=elev, hour="morning", seed=3, pool=pool)
        panels.append((lab, img))
    # deer groups
    for lab, kind, k, elev in [("blacktail doe with twins, iso 30, 12 px/m", "doe_twins", 12, 30), ("blacktails bounding away, landscape, 10 px/m", "flee", 10, 8)]:
        mem = herd.deer_group(kind, seed=2, heading=20)
        panels.append((lab, herd.scene(mem, k, 200, 110, elev=elev, hour="morning", seed=4, pool=pool)))
    Z = 3
    f = _font(12)
    W = max(p.shape[1] for _, p in panels) * Z + 20
    H = sum(p.shape[0] * Z + 28 for _, p in panels) + 40
    im = Image.new("RGB", (W, H), (24, 24, 28))
    d = ImageDraw.Draw(im)
    d.text((8, 8), "G. herds: arrangement x distance x camera (Roosevelt cow-calf herd of 26: ~17 cows, 7 calves, 2-3 yearlings; composition after the Olympic herd plan)", fill=(236, 236, 236), font=f)
    y = 34
    for lab, p in panels:
        d.text((10, y), lab, fill=(190, 190, 200), font=f)
        im.paste(Image.fromarray(p.repeat(Z, 0).repeat(Z, 1)), (10, y + 18))
        y += p.shape[0] * Z + 28
    im.save(os.path.join(OUT, "G_herds.png"))
    print("G_herds.png", im.size)

    with Pool(4) as pool:

            globals()["sheet_" + w](pool)

c=open('coat.py').read()
c=c.replace("PRIORITY = [NOSE, EYE, TAILTOP]","PRIORITY = [NOSE, EYE, TAILTOP, SPOT]")
c=c.replace('''def materials(ind, tb, p, n, u, cth, sth):''','''def spots(x, cth, sth, seed, xr, xf, rows=((0.72, 0.0), (0.5, 0.5), (0.28, 0.0), (0.08, 0.5)), dx=0.075, r=0.022):
    """Fawn and calf spots: dots in rows along the back, each row offset half a step, spaced
    ~0.075 H, radius ~0.022 H; a little jitter per dot (deterministic in seed)."""
    ang = np.arctan2(sth, cth)                 # 0 at the spine top, +-pi at the belly
    out = np.zeros(len(x), bool)
    for k, (cl, off) in enumerate(rows):
        a0 = np.arccos(np.clip(cl, -1, 1))      # the row's angle from the spine
        for sgn in (1, -1):
            along = (x - xr) / dx + off
            i = np.round(along)
            jit = (_hash(i, k * 7 + (sgn > 0), seed) - 0.5) * 0.5
            d_al = (along - i - jit) * dx
            d_ac = (np.abs(ang) - a0) * 0.18       # arc length ~ angle x radius (~0.18 H)
            hit = (d_al ** 2 + d_ac ** 2 < r * r) & (np.sign(ang + 1e-9) == sgn) & (x > xr + 0.1) & (x < xf - 0.14)
            hit &= _hash(i, k * 13 + (sgn > 0), seed + 1) < 0.85
            out |= hit
    return out


def materials(ind, tb, p, n, u, cth, sth):''')
c=c.replace('''                out[out == DARK] = BODY
                h = _hash(x * 22, (np.arctan2(sth, cth)) * 5.5, ind.seed)
                spots = (up > 0.15) & (up < 0.9) & (x > xr + 0.15) & (x < xf - 0.12) & (h < 0.33)
                out[spots] = SPOT''','''                out[out == DARK] = BODY
                out[spots(x, cth, sth, ind.seed, xr, xf)] = SPOT''')
c=c.replace('''            if young:
                h = _hash(x * 26, (np.arctan2(sth, cth)) * 6.0, ind.seed)
                spots = (up > 0.1) & (up < 0.92) & (x > xr + 0.08) & (x < xf - 0.1) & (h < 0.36)
                out[spots] = SPOT''','''            if young:
                out[spots(x, cth, sth, ind.seed, xr, xf, dx=0.07, r=0.02)] = SPOT''')
open('coat.py','w').write(c)
p=open('palette.py').read()

open('ground.py','w').write(g)

Bash: Add body contact shadow
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/elements/deer-elk/src && python3 - <<'EOF'
s=open('paint.py').read()
s=s.replace('''def value_field(R, view, tubes):''','''def contact_shadow(R, tubes, view, k):
    """The occlusion shadow under the body, whatever the sun: the torso's footprint on the
    ground, a little narrower than the body (the animal must sit ON the meadow)."""
    f = render.view_xf(view)
    tor = [t for t in tubes if t.name == "torso"][0]
    ox, oy = R.origin
    H, W = R.cov.shape
    c = tor.c
    n = len(c)
    u = np.linspace(0.12, 0.88, 24)
    idx = u * (n - 1)
    cx = np.interp(idx, np.arange(n), c[:, 0])
    cy = np.interp(idx, np.arange(n), c[:, 1])
    hw = np.interp(idx, np.arange(n), tor.b) * 0.95
    pts = []
    for s in np.linspace(-1, 1, 9):
        pts.append(np.stack([cx, cy + s * hw * np.sqrt(1 - (2 * u - 1) ** 6 * 0.9), np.zeros_like(cx)], 1))
    P = np.concatenate(pts)
    sx, sv, d = f(P)
    X = np.round(sx * k + ox).astype(int)
    Y = np.round(oy - sv * k).astype(int)
    m = np.zeros((H, W), bool)
    ok = (X >= 0) & (X < W) & (Y >= 0) & (Y < H)
    m[Y[ok], X[ok]] = True
    from scipy import ndimage as ndi
    m = ndi.binary_closing(m, iterations=1) | m
    R.ground_shadow = np.maximum(R.ground_shadow, m.astype(float))
    return R


def value_field(R, view, tubes):''')
s=s.replace('''    R.k = k
    import hide, fringe''','''    R.k = k
    R = contact_shadow(R, tubes, view, k)
    import hide, fringe''')
open('paint.py','w').write(s)

h=h.replace('''def screen_light(view):''','''def hair_quant(x, seed=0):
    """Ordered dither whose cells are 1 px wide and 2 px tall, staggered per column: the mixed
    tone between two steps becomes short vertical dashes, the hair lying down the flank,
    instead of the rock checker. (Ferrari: ordered dither for hard things, organic marks for
    organic ones; this keeps the order and gives it the hair's direction.)"""
    H, W = x.shape
    yy, xx = np.mgrid[0:H, 0:W]
    ph = ((xx * 7919 + seed * 104729) >> 3) & 1          # per-column phase: stagger the dashes
    t = (BAYER[((yy + ph) // 2) % 2, xx % 2] + 0.5) / 4
    fl = np.floor(x)
    return np.clip(fl + ((x - fl) > t), 0, 5).astype(np.int32)


def screen_light(view):''')
h=h.replace('''    if size_px >= 28:
        R.step = bayer_quant(R.x)''','''    if size_px >= 28:
        R.step = hair_quant(R.x) if hair else bayer_quant(R.x)''')
h=h.replace("def apply(R, view, tubes, size_px, flatten=0.5, dark_mats=None):","def apply(R, view, tubes, size_px, flatten=0.5, dark_mats=None, hair=True):")
open('hide.py','w').write(h)

"""photo (area-reduced to the same size) | mine | Ferrari's warm boulder (V19), all at 8x."""
import numpy as np, sys
from PIL import Image
import rig, paint, lw
from render import View
from board import BOARD
tag = sys.argv[1]
Z = 6
d = lw.load('V19'); fer = d['pal'][d['idx'][245:300, 225:290]]
rows = []
for i, (f, crop, gr, wr, (spn, sex), g, ph, yaw) in enumerate(BOARD):
    L = 60
    im = Image.open(f).convert('RGB').crop(crop)
    s = (L / 1.62) / (gr - wr)
    p = np.array(im.resize((max(1, int(im.width * s)), max(1, int(im.height * s))), Image.BOX))
    ind = rig.Individual(spn, sex, 'adult', 'summer' if spn == 'deer' else 'autumn', 1)
    R, _, k = paint.paint_index(ind, g, ph, View(yaw=yaw + (-14 if yaw == 0 else 14), elev=6, sun_el=45, sun_az=-155 if yaw == 0 else -25), size_px=L)
    m = paint.colour(R, ind, 'morning')
    rows.append([p, m, fer])
for i, r in enumerate(rows):
    H = max(a.shape[0] for a in r)
    out = np.full((H, sum(a.shape[1] + 4 for a in r), 3), 24, np.uint8)
    x = 0
    for a in r:
        out[H - a.shape[0]:, x:x + a.shape[1]] = a; x += a.shape[1] + 4
    Image.fromarray(out.repeat(Z, 0).repeat(Z, 1)).save(f'../stages/s1/trip_{tag}_{i}.png')
EOF

        if m not in t:
            t[m] = t[rig.BODY]
    if ind.season in ("winter", "spring") and ind.age != "young":
        # winter coat: thicker, greyer and duller (elk paler, deer grey-brown); spring: the
        # ragged moult, dull and a little darker
        cs = 0.62 if ind.season == "winter" else 0.8
        lk = 1.04 if (ind.season == "winter" and ind.species == "elk") else (0.96 if ind.season == "spring" else 1.0)
        for m in (rig.BODY, rig.DARK, rig.LEG, rig.BELLY, rig.PALE):
            d, l = t[m]
            t[m] = (desat(d, cs, 1.0), desat(l, cs, lk))
    return t


def desat(c, s, lk=1.0):
    lab = to_lab(c)
    lab[1:] *= s
    lab[0] *= lk
    return to_rgb(lab)''')
open('palette.py','w').write(p)
